*Hisakaki Kosame was standing on the shrine grounds.*
*To wait for the intruder. To make her premonition into reality.*
*If too much time passed, she would be noticed by others.*
*In particular, if Kosame's older sister Komomo found out, it would only cause her undue anxiety.*
*That's what truly weighed on Kosame's heart.*
*So Kosame had no room for error. Her impatience turned into sweat, dripping in cold beads down her brow.*
Kosame: "It's... awfully cold..."
*The night breeze was cold, and it cut through her clothes.*
*Kosame already knew that such a sensation was a mere phantom born of past experiences.*
*But it also represented one more memory inside her.*
*Kosame was thankful to her sister for those precious memories which gave rise to phantoms equivalent to reality.*
*It was her sister that helped Kosame to be reborn into this world.*
Kosame: "I'm here today because of Nee-san..."
*That's why she wanted to protect her sister.*
*Not only her sister. She wanted to protect her entire family.*
*And her mother who always smiled and goofed around.*
*And her serious but loyal and reliable father.*
*As well as Setsuna-senpai.*
*She couldn't allow her family to be put in danger because of her.*
*For the sake of all the people who protected her, she decided that this time she would protect all of them.*
*There was one thing that she simply had to do in order to accomplish that.*
Kosame: "Please... hurry up..."
*She wished for this person to show herself soon.*
*So that her family could continue living on this land.*
*So that she could protect the place where they all lived.*
*So that she could make that dream come true.*
Kosame: "Asuka Ima-"
*A response was given in the form of one flash of light.*
*Several strands of hair were carried away with the blade.*
*Each strand fluttering on the cold breeze reflected clearly in Kosame's eyes.*
*She felt as if time was flowing at a leisurely pace.*
*It was only by channeling that feeling into her body that Kosame was able to twist and dodge the flash of light.*
*It was a practiced movement.*
*She refused to return to the stars just yet.*
*She still had things to do.*
*It was that strong determination which saved her.*
*Unbound by conventional physics, Kosame displayed a reaction time she owed to the otherworldly physics governing her personal nature.*
*It was a special technique unique to astral spirits and astral deities which allowed her to trace vectors of thought back to their origin.*
Kosame: "...I was waiting for you."
*A shadow moved at the edge of her vision.*
*Her guest had arrived.*
*Two scarlet maidens faced off under the moon-*
Kosame: "Is that so..."
Kosame: "Did that light come from a bow and arrow?"
*Ima's expression did not budge as she notched another arrow and pulled back the bowstring.*
*Kosame did not need to examine that bow to know that it was a holy relic.*
*The arrowhead refused to let Kosame escape.*
*The relic extended its tentacles out to bind her body, preventing her from slipping into the moonlight to hide from human eyes.*
Ima: "I heard that you were the remnants of an astral deity."
Ima: "At the very least, you couldn't be less than a high level astral spirit..."
Kosame: "...Who knows? I'm actually quite familiar with someone even stronger than myself."
Kosame: "Someone with the body of a human who has a very powerful spirit that they can turn into power..."
Kosame: "There are four such people in this land who defy what you think of as common sense."
Ima: "Suwa Setsuna, Hisakaki Mayaka, and the other two would be..."
Kosame: "Nee-san and Kogasaka-san."
Ima: "..."
Kosame: "My precious friend, named Kogasaka You, is probably not anyone you are aware of, though..."
Kosame: "Aren't you fully aware of Nee-san?"
Ima: "...The shrine maiden who raised you, if I'm not mistaken."
*Ima responded with indifference.*
*Kosame considered it a blessing that Ima didn't wordlessly continue her attack.*
Ima: "I've heard that your sister is the only person currently working as a shrine maiden who has succeeded in that ritual."

Kosame: "Perhaps you have some other business in this land besides simply sending me back to the stars."

*Ima's expression didn't even budge.*
*That blank expression was different from Setsuna's wise impartiality. If anything, it was closer to Komomo's attitude.*
*It was reminiscent of Komomo's face when she got angry and started to give curt, matter-of-fact answers.*
*Not only when she was mad. Komomo also chose to kill her emotions in this way when she was sad, grieving, or otherwise emotionally imbalanced.*
*Kosame had often provoked her sister on purpose in such a situation just to get a response.*
*She figured that it would be the same with Ima.*
*Kosame perceived that there was some kind of intense discord inside Ima's heart, which forced her to kill all her emotions.*

*She could see the fissure from here.*

Ima: "I had no intention of making a deal with you in the first place."
*Kosame's body melted into the moonlight.*
*She could only choose to withdraw from this place. Since the situation showed no sign of favorable progress, she had no reason to stay and risk being returned by the shrine maiden.*
*Ima's pointed glare tried to follow Kosame's movement.*
*But she wasn't fast enough.*
*As her mind's eye had not yet opened, her heart unready for what it might show her, she could not follow that movement.*
*Kosame disappeared.*
*Kosame frustrated the other's visual senses, allowing her to see naught but illusions.*
*It was a natural phenomenon equal to the moon blinding one's eyes with its brilliance.*
*So it was impossible for Ima to escape the effects of that phenomenon with her human eyes.*
*With the moon so full in the sky, the relic's light was lacking-*
*A flash of light erupted from Ima's hand.*
*She had loosed an arrow to chase after Kosame's shadow, but the arrowhead found itself meaninglessly embedded in the shrine's roof.*
*Kosame had already slipped into the moonlight to make her escape.*
*It was impossible for Ima's bow and arrow to capture her, now that she had disappeared from sight.*

*Kosame had left her shrine maiden's outfit behind to shroud her trail.*
*No one could hope to touch Kosame's body without the aid of their sight. The arrow was bound to miss before it was fired.*

Ima: "...How futile."
*Ima pulled the bowstring back and loosed another arrow.*
*Not only one.*

*A volley of surging light in every direction.*

*This violent display embedded arrows in trees all around.*

Ima: "Why did I show myself before you?"
Ima: "Why did I dare to show myself...?"
Ima: "Why did I remain in this land?"
Ima: "It was because I still had a chance of victory. There's no other answer."
*The arrows rained down wildly in every direction.*

Ima: "Regardless, that was not your real mistake."

*When the volley fire came to an end, silence returned to the shrine grounds.*

Ima: "More accurately, they are not reflected. The goal is to amplify the wavelength."

Ima: "This was a bit more troublesome than expected."
Ima: "However, I will not allow you to escape."
Ima: "Absolutely not..."
???: "You must think this is a one-on-one fight."
*Almost at the exact same time as that voice rang out.*

*Her clothes fluttered in the wind as she jumped from that spot, preparing for the new challenger.*
Setsuna: "You chose to retreat from me. That was a good choice."

Ima: "..."
Setsuna: "Do you have some personal grudge against astral spirits?"
*Ima remained silent.*
*The fresh glinting of the arrowhead expressed all the hostility which Ima's mouth did not.*
Setsuna: "A bow suits you, as one with such superb spiritual powers."
Setsuna: "Since you can use your intuition to aim for an enemy, even after they've disappeared from your sight."

Ima: "..."
Setsuna: "I'm sure you already understand that your intuition will not save you from me either."

*But in this case, her poker face seemed feigned.*

Setsuna: "The difference between us is a matter of experience."
Setsuna: "For someone as young as you, there is no hope of competing with me. It's just a matter of seniority."

Ima: "..."
Setsuna: "Like a stubborn young girl in the spring of her life."
Ima: "...!"
*A bolt flew from Ima's hand.*
Setsuna: "How dull."
*Setsuna easily struck that arrow down with her naginata.*
*In that moment, Ima turned and dashed away.*
*She headed toward the wooded path behind the shrine.*
Kosame: "Ima-san...!"
*Kosame yelled out to her without thinking.*
Kosame: "Do you remember the name Asuka Mirai...?!"
*Ima's feet slowed.*
Ima: "...I've thrown away my memories."
*It was a quiet voice, but the night breeze certainly did carry those words to Kosame's ears.*
Kosame: "What does that mean..."
Setsuna: "You mean that you are ashamed of your past self? Although I can understand why one would want to abandon their past self."
*Ima's form disappeared into the darkness.*
Kosame: "Ima-san...!"
*It wasn't clear if Kosame's shout even reached Ima's ears.*
*Setsuna didn't chase after Ima.*
*Kosame was convinced that it was her own fault.*

Setsuna: "...I guess she won't rise to cheap taunts."
*She murmured and sighed.*
Setsuna: "It also seems that the emotions she deadened inside herself won't be easily awoken."

*Setsuna raised her hand up.*
*Kosame flinched and closed her eyes.*
